Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出将人类心理建模为操作系统，构建包含需求状态空间的认知架构，将通用人工智能形式化为在特定需求空间中进行最优决策的系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在通过人类心理建模的方法来构建人工通用智能系统，解决如何让智能体在考虑其生物或存在意义的需求下进行经验学习的问题。

Method: 提出认知架构概念，将心理视为操作系统，包含状态空间（特别是需求状态）、智能作为决策系统；提出计算形式化方法，让智能体在包含需求的状态空间中进行经验学习；将AGI构建形式化为在特定需求空间中进行最优决策的问题。

Result: 提出了一个概念框架和计算形式化方法，并展示了该模型的最小实验实现。

Conclusion: 通过将人类心理建模为操作系统，并将AGI构建形式化为在需求空间中的最优决策问题，为构建具有生物或存在意义的智能体提供了理论框架和实现路径。

Abstract: This article presents an overview of approaches to modeling the human psyche in the context of constructing an artificial one. Based on this overview, a concept of cognitive architecture is proposed, in which the psyche is viewed as the operating system of a living or artificial subject, comprising a space of states, including the state of needs that determine the meaning of a subject's being in relation to stimuli from the external world, and intelligence as a decision-making system regarding actions in this world to satisfy these needs. Based on this concept, a computational formalization is proposed for creating artificial general intelligence systems for an agent through experiential learning in a state space that includes agent's needs, taking into account their biological or existential significance for the intelligent agent, along with agent's sensations and actions. Thus, the problem of constructing artificial general intelligence is formalized as a system for making optimal decisions in the space of specific agent needs under conditions of uncertainty, maximizing success in achieving goals, minimizing existential risks, and maximizing energy efficiency. A minimal experimental implementation of the model is presented.

</details>


### [77] [Do Metrics for Counterfactual Explanations Align with User Perception?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15607)
*Felix Liedeker,Basil Ell,Philipp Cimiano,Christoph Düsing*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 当前反事实解释的评估指标与人类对解释质量的感知相关性较弱，且高度依赖数据集，表明需要更以人为中心的评估方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释性对可信AI系统至关重要，但当前评估反事实解释的指标多为算法评估指标，很少验证其与人类对解释质量判断的一致性，这引发了对这些指标是否真正反映用户感知的疑问

Method: 通过实证研究直接比较算法评估指标与人类判断，参与者从多个维度评估反事实解释的质量，并将这些评估与一套全面的标准反事实指标相关联，分析个体关系以及指标组合预测人类评估的能力

Result: 算法指标与人类评分之间的相关性普遍较弱且高度依赖数据集；增加预测模型中使用的指标数量并不能可靠地改善预测效果，表明当前指标在捕捉与人类相关的标准方面存在结构性限制

Conclusion: 广泛使用的反事实评估指标未能反映用户感知的解释质量关键方面，强调需要更以人为中心的方法来评估可解释人工智能

Abstract: Explainability is widely regarded as essential for trustworthy artificial intelligence systems. However, the metrics commonly used to evaluate counterfactual explanations are algorithmic evaluation metrics that are rarely validated against human judgments of explanation quality. This raises the question of whether such metrics meaningfully reflect user perceptions. We address this question through an empirical study that directly compares algorithmic evaluation metrics with human judgments across three datasets. Participants rated counterfactual explanations along multiple dimensions of perceived quality, which we relate to a comprehensive set of standard counterfactual metrics. We analyze both individual relationships and the extent to which combinations of metrics can predict human assessments. Our results show that correlations between algorithmic metrics and human ratings are generally weak and strongly dataset-dependent. Moreover, increasing the number of metrics used in predictive models does not lead to reliable improvements, indicating structural limitations in how current metrics capture criteria relevant for humans. Overall, our findings suggest that widely used counterfactual evaluation metrics fail to reflect key aspects of explanation quality as perceived by users, underscoring the need for more human-centered approaches to evaluating explainable artificial intelligence.

</details>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [78] [A Hybrid Tsallis-Polarization Impurity Measure for Decision Trees: Theoretical Foundations and Empirical Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13241)
*Edouard Lansiaux,Idriss Jairi,Hayfa Zgaya-Biau*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出ITC（集成Tsallis组合）作为决策树学习的混合不纯度度量，结合归一化Tsallis熵与指数极化分量，在理论严谨性与计算效率间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不纯度度量往往在理论严谨性和计算效率之间做出妥协，要么牺牲理论严谨性以获得计算效率，要么反之。需要一种既能保持数学原理性又能平衡两方面需求的度量方法。

Method: 提出ITC混合不纯度度量，核心创新在于Tsallis熵的信息论基础与极化分量对分布不对称性敏感性的互补性。该方法具有三个可调参数（α, β, γ），计算复杂度为O(K)。建立了关键理论特性：在明确参数条件下的凹性、适当的边界条件以及与经典度量的联系。

Result: 在7个基准数据集上对23种不纯度度量进行五折重复比较评估，简单参数化度量（Tsallis α=0.5）获得最高平均准确率（91.17%），ITC变体获得竞争性结果（88.38-89.16%）。Friedman检验（χ²=3.89, p=0.692）显示顶级性能度量间无显著全局差异。ITC的优势在于其坚实的理论基础：在合适条件下的可证明凹性、灵活参数化和计算效率。

Conclusion: ITC提供了一个数学原理性的框架，平衡了理论严谨性和计算效率。其价值在于坚实的理论基础、灵活的参数化和计算效率，使其成为理论保证至关重要时的严格、可推广的替代方案。提供了基于应用优先级的度量选择指南，并发布了开源实现以促进可重复性和进一步研究。

Abstract: We introduce the Integrated Tsallis Combination (ITC), a hybrid impurity measure for decision tree learning that combines normalized Tsallis entropy with an exponential polarization component. While many existing measures sacrifice theoretical soundness for computational efficiency or vice versa, ITC provides a mathematically principled framework that balances both aspects. The core innovation lies in the complementarity between Tsallis entropy's information-theoretic foundations and the polarization component's sensitivity to distributional asymmetry. We establish key theoretical properties-concavity under explicit parameter conditions, proper boundary conditions, and connections to classical measures-and provide a rigorous justification for the hybridization strategy. Through an extensive comparative evaluation on seven benchmark datasets comparing 23 impurity measures with five-fold repetition, we show that simple parametric measures (Tsallis $α=0.5$) achieve the highest average accuracy ($91.17\%$), while ITC variants yield competitive results ($88.38-89.16\%$) with strong theoretical guarantees. Statistical analysis (Friedman test: $χ^2=3.89$, $p=0.692$) reveals no significant global differences among top performers, indicating practical equivalence for many applications. ITC's value resides in its solid theoretical grounding-proven concavity under suitable conditions, flexible parameterization ($α$, $β$, $γ$), and computational efficiency $O(K)$-making it a rigorous, generalizable alternative when theoretical guarantees are paramount. We provide guidelines for measure selection based on application priorities and release an open-source implementation to foster reproducibility and further research.

</details>


### [79] [Filtered Spectral Projection for Quantum Principal Component Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13441)
*Sk Mujaffar Hossain,Satadeep Bhattacharjee*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出FSPA算法，在量子主成分分析中绕过显式特征值估计，直接投影到主导谱子空间，保持下游任务性能稳定


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统qPCA通常提取密度算子的特征值和特征向量，但在许多实际应用中，目标只是将数据投影到主导谱子空间。显式特征值估计可能不必要且计算成本高

Method: 提出Filtered Spectral Projection Algorithm (FSPA)：投影优先框架，放大与主导主成分子空间的非零初始重叠，在小间隙和近简并情况下保持鲁棒性，避免无偏情况下的虚假对称破缺

Result: 对于振幅编码的中心化数据，系综密度矩阵与协方差矩阵一致；对于非中心化数据，ρ对应无中心化的PCA，并推导了特征值交错界来量化与标准PCA的偏差；数值实验显示当下游投影质量保持时性能稳定

Conclusion: 在广泛的qPCA设置中，谱投影是基本原语，显式特征值估计通常不必要；FSPA为量子机器学习提供了更高效的替代方案

Abstract: Quantum principal component analysis (qPCA) is commonly formulated as the extraction of eigenvalues and eigenvectors of a covariance-encoded density operator. Yet in many qPCA settings, the practical objective is simpler: projecting data onto the dominant spectral subspace. In this work, we introduce a projection-first framework, the Filtered Spectral Projection Algorithm (FSPA), which bypasses explicit eigenvalue estimation while preserving the essential spectral structure. FSPA amplifies any nonzero warm-start overlap with the leading principal subspace and remains robust in small-gap and near-degenerate regimes without inducing artificial symmetry breaking in the absence of bias. To connect this approach to classical datasets, we show that for amplitude-encoded centered data, the ensemble density matrix $ρ=\sum_i p_i|ψ_i\rangle\langleψ_i|$ coincides with the covariance matrix. For uncentered data, $ρ$ corresponds to PCA without centering, and we derive eigenvalue interlacing bounds quantifying the deviation from standard PCA. We further show that ensembles of quantum states admit an equivalent centered covariance interpretation. Numerical demonstrations on benchmark datasets, including Breast Cancer Wisconsin and handwritten Digits, show that downstream performance remains stable whenever projection quality is preserved. These results suggest that, in a broad class of qPCA settings, spectral projection is the essential primitive, and explicit eigenvalue estimation is often unnecessary.

</details>


### [80] [Standard Acquisition Is Sufficient for Asynchronous Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13501)
*Ben Riegler,James Odgers,Vincent Fortuin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 异步贝叶斯优化中，标准采集函数（如上置信边界）的理论保证与顺序汤普森采样相当，无需复杂多样性强制方法，中间后验更新足以避免冗余查询


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战异步贝叶斯优化领域的基本前提：现有方法认为标准采集函数会导致冗余和重复查询，因此提出复杂的多样性强制解决方案。本文质疑这一前提，旨在证明标准方法实际上具有与顺序方法相当的理论保证

Method: 对异步贝叶斯优化进行概念分析，揭示现有方法忽略了中间后验更新的重要性。通过理论分析证明标准采集函数（如上置信边界）在异步设置下具有与顺序汤普森采样相当的理论保证。进行广泛的实验验证

Result: 理论分析表明中间后验更新通常足以避免冗余查询。实验证明简单的标准采集函数在合成和真实世界任务中与专门设计的异步方法表现相当或更优。多样性强制方法在异步设置中可能因惩罚繁忙位置而导致过度探索，降低性能

Conclusion: 异步贝叶斯优化中无需复杂的多样性强制方法，标准采集函数具有充分的理论保证和实际性能。中间后验更新是避免冗余查询的关键机制，现有方法对此的忽视导致了对复杂解决方案的不必要追求

Abstract: Asynchronous Bayesian optimization is widely used for gradient-free optimization in domains with independent parallel experiments and varying evaluation times. Existing methods posit that standard acquisitions lead to redundant and repeated queries, proposing complex solutions to enforce diversity in queries. Challenging this fundamental premise, we show that methods, like the Upper Confidence Bound, can in fact achieve theoretical guarantees essentially equivalent to those of sequential Thompson sampling. A conceptual analysis of asynchronous Bayesian optimization reveals that existing works neglect intermediate posterior updates, which we find to be generally sufficient to avoid redundant queries. Further investigation shows that by penalizing busy locations, diversity-enforcing methods can over-explore in asynchronous settings, reducing their performance. Our extensive experiments demonstrate that simple standard acquisition functions match or outperform purpose-built asynchronous methods across synthetic and real-world tasks.

</details>


### [81] [Holographic Invariant Storage: Design-Time Safety Contracts via Vector Symbolic Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13558)
*Arsenios Scrivens*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: HIS协议利用双极向量符号架构特性，为LLM上下文漂移缓解提供设计时安全合约，提供三个闭式保证：单信号恢复保真度约0.707、连续噪声鲁棒性、多信号容量衰减，支持系统工程师在设计阶段预算恢复保真度和码本容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决LLM上下文漂移问题，现有基于计时器或嵌入距离度量的方法无法在设计阶段提供可量化的安全保证，需要一种能够在部署前评估和预算恢复性能的系统化方法。

Method: 提出全息不变存储（HIS）协议，整合双极向量符号架构的已知特性，形成设计时安全合约。通过蒙特卡洛模拟（n=1000）验证理论边界，并进行行为实验（4个LLM，2B-7B参数，720次试验）测试安全重注入效果。

Result: 理论分析提供三个闭式保证：单信号恢复保真度收敛于1/√2≈0.707（与噪声深度和内容无关）、连续噪声鲁棒性2Φ(1/σ)-1、多信号容量衰减≈√1/(K+1)。行为实验表明安全重注入在2B规模下提高了依从性。

Conclusion: HIS协议为LLM上下文漂移缓解提供了首个设计时可评估的安全合约，使系统工程师能够在部署前预算恢复保真度和码本容量，这是计时器或嵌入距离度量无法提供的特性，为LLM安全应用提供了理论基础和实用工具。

Abstract: We introduce Holographic Invariant Storage (HIS), a protocol that assembles known properties of bipolar Vector Symbolic Architectures into a design-time safety contract for LLM context-drift mitigation. The contract provides three closed-form guarantees evaluable before deployment: single-signal recovery fidelity converging to $1/\sqrt{2} \approx 0.707$ (regardless of noise depth or content), continuous-noise robustness $2Φ(1/σ) - 1$, and multi-signal capacity degradation $\approx\sqrt{1/(K+1)}$. These bounds, validated by Monte Carlo simulation ($n = 1{,}000$), enable a systems engineer to budget recovery fidelity and codebook capacity at design time -- a property no timer or embedding-distance metric provides. A pilot behavioral experiment (four LLMs, 2B--7B, 720 trials) confirms that safety re-injection improves adherence at the 2B scale; full results are in an appendix.

</details>


### [82] [Robust Automatic Differentiation of Square-Root Kalman Filters via Gramian Differentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13559)
*Adrien Corenflos*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种用于平方根卡尔曼滤波中QR分解梯度计算的新方法，通过Gramian恒等式避免了传统方法在秩不足或半正交因子非唯一时的梯度问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平方根卡尔曼滤波在状态空间模型参数学习中需要计算QR分解的梯度，但传统方法存在两个问题：1) 当m>n时半正交因子非唯一导致梯度未定义；2) 标准雅可比公式涉及逆矩阵，在输入矩阵秩不足时会发散。这些问题阻碍了基于梯度的参数学习。

Method: 提出基于Gramian恒等式MM^⊤的链式法则：观察到所有与学习相关的滤波器输出仅依赖于输入矩阵的Gramian，因此即使三角化本身不光滑，复合损失函数在M上也是光滑的。推导了该恒等式的闭式微分链式法则，并扩展到秩不足输入的情况，采用两部分分解：基于Moore-Penrose伪逆的列空间项和用于M列空间外扰动的零空间修正项。

Result: 证明了该方法对卡尔曼对数边际似然和滤波矩是精确的，能够处理秩不足输入，避免了传统梯度计算中的发散问题，为平方根卡尔曼滤波的梯度学习提供了稳定可靠的解决方案。

Conclusion: 通过Gramian恒等式方法，解决了平方根卡尔曼滤波中QR分解梯度计算的数值稳定性问题，为状态空间模型的参数学习提供了有效的梯度计算框架，特别适用于秩不足和半正交因子非唯一的情况。

Abstract: Square-root Kalman filters propagate state covariances in Cholesky-factor form for numerical stability, and are a natural target for gradient-based parameter learning in state-space models. Their core operation, triangularization of a matrix $M \in \mathbb{R}^{n \times m}$, is computed via a QR decomposition in practice, but naively differentiating through it causes two problems: the semi-orthogonal factor is non-unique when $m > n$, yielding undefined gradients; and the standard Jacobian formula involves inverses, which diverges when $M$ is rank-deficient. Both are resolved by the observation that all filter outputs relevant to learning depend on the input matrix only through the Gramian $MM^\top$, so the composite loss is smooth in $M$ even where the triangularization is not. We derive a closed-form chain-rule directly from the differential of this Gramian identity, prove it exact for the Kalman log-marginal likelihood and filtered moments, and extend it to rank-deficient inputs via a two-component decomposition: a column-space term based on the Moore--Penrose pseudoinverse, and a null-space correction for perturbations outside the column space of $M$.

</details>


### [83] [Robust Sequential Tracking via Bounded Information Geometry and Non-Parametric Field Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13613)
*Carlos C. Rodriguez*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种解决标准序列推断架构在处理极端结构化异常值时出现的归一化危机的方法，通过非参数场和严格不变的信息分离在统计流形上物理截断空间分布的无限尾部。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准序列推断架构在处理极端结构化异常值时面临归一化危机，因为它们在无界参数空间上操作，缺乏适当分离异常值所需的内在几何结构，导致无界协方差膨胀和均值发散。

Method: 在元先验层面分析推断的抽象序列，通过在无限维空间上极值化动作，使用由预先验锚定的非参数场，并利用统计流形上的严格不变Delta（或ν）信息分离来物理截断空间分布的无限尾部。

Result: 该方法将活动参数空间压缩为严格有限、可归一化的概率"液滴"，在LiDAR机动目标跟踪、高频加密货币订单流和量子态层析三个领域的实证基准测试中，证明了这种有界信息几何能够解析地截断异常值。

Conclusion: 通过有界信息几何方法，可以在不依赖无限尾部分布假设的情况下确保鲁棒估计，解决了标准推断架构在处理极端异常值时的结构失效问题。

Abstract: Standard sequential inference architectures are compromised by a normalizability crisis when confronted with extreme, structured outliers. By operating on unbounded parameter spaces, state-of-the-art estimators lack the intrinsic geometry required to appropriately sever anomalies, resulting in unbounded covariance inflation and mean divergence. This paper resolves this structural failure by analyzing the abstraction sequence of inference at the meta-prior level (S_2). We demonstrate that extremizing the action over an infinite-dimensional space requires a non-parametric field anchored by a pre-prior, as a uniform volume element mathematically does not exist. By utilizing strictly invariant Delta (or ν) Information Separations on the statistical manifold, we physically truncate the infinite tails of the spatial distribution. When evaluated as a Radon-Nikodym derivative against the base measure, the active parameter space compresses into a strictly finite, normalizable probability droplet. Empirical benchmarks across three domains--LiDAR maneuvering target tracking, high-frequency cryptocurrency order flow, and quantum state tomography--demonstrate that this bounded information geometry analytically truncates outliers, ensuring robust estimation without relying on infinite-tailed distributional assumptions.

</details>


### [84] [When Should Humans Step In? Optimal Human Dispatching in AI-Assisted Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13688)
*Lezhi Tan,Naomi Sagan,Lihua Lei,Jose Blanchet*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一个决策理论框架用于人机协作，将AI评估视为因子级信号，人类判断作为可选择性获取的成本信息，通过优化人类努力分配来修正AI输出，在同行评审应用中仅用20-30%的人类信息达到接近全人工评审的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统在辅助人类决策时产生的评估往往存在噪声或系统性偏差，需要解决如何最有效地分配有限的人类努力来修正AI输出，以优化最终决策质量。

Method: 提出一个通用决策理论框架，将AI评估视为因子级信号，人类判断作为可选择性获取的成本信息。将最优选择问题转化为最大化候选因子子集的奖励，将策略设计转化为奖励估计。开发了非参数和线性模型下的估计程序，涵盖上下文和非上下文选择规则。

Result: 在AI辅助同行评审应用中，该方法显著优于仅使用LLM的预测，仅使用20-30%的人类信息就能达到与全人工评审相当的性能。在线性模型下，最优规则具有闭式解，具有因子重要性和残差方差的清晰解释。

Conclusion: 研究结果既凸显了人类干预的价值，也展示了基于原则的分配方法的效率。在线性模型下推导的简单规则能显著降低计算成本而不损害最终预测性能，为实际应用提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: AI systems increasingly assist human decision making by producing preliminary assessments of complex inputs. However, such AI-generated assessments can often be noisy or systematically biased, raising a central question: how should costly human effort be allocated to correct AI outputs where it matters the most for the final decision? We propose a general decision-theoretic framework for human-AI collaboration in which AI assessments are treated as factor-level signals and human judgments as costly information that can be selectively acquired. We consider cases where the optimal selection problem reduces to maximizing a reward associated with each candidate subset of factors, and turn policy design into reward estimation. We develop estimation procedures under both nonparametric and linear models, covering contextual and non-contextual selection rules. In the linear setting, the optimal rule admits a closed-form expression with a clear interpretation in terms of factor importance and residual variance. We apply our framework to AI-assisted peer review. Our approach substantially outperforms LLM-only predictions and achieves performance comparable to full human review while using only 20-30% of the human information. Across different selection rules, we find that simpler rules derived under linear models can significantly reduce computational cost without harming final prediction performance. Our results highlight both the value of human intervention and the efficiency of principled dispatching.

</details>


### [85] [An Interpretable and Stable Framework for Sparse Principal Component Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13806)
*Ying Hu,Hu Yang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: SP-SPCA方法通过引入均衡参数自适应调整变量惩罚，在高维噪声数据中优于传统稀疏主成分分析


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统稀疏主成分分析(SPCA)对变量施加统一惩罚，未考虑变量重要性差异，在高度噪声或结构复杂场景中性能不稳定

Method: 提出SP-SPCA方法，在正则化框架中引入单一均衡参数，自适应调整变量惩罚，修改L2惩罚以灵活控制稀疏性和解释方差的权衡

Result: 模拟研究显示该方法在识别稀疏载荷模式、过滤噪声变量和保持累积方差方面优于标准稀疏主成分方法，特别是在高维噪声场景；实际犯罪和金融市场数据应用验证了其有效性

Conclusion: SP-SPCA为复杂高维数据稀疏建模提供了稳健高效替代方案，在稳定性、特征选择和可解释性方面具有明显优势

Abstract: Sparse principal component analysis (SPCA) addresses the poor interpretability and variable redundancy often encountered by principal component analysis (PCA) in high-dimensional data. However, SPCA typically imposes uniform penalties on variables and does not account for differences in variable importance, which may lead to unstable performance in highly noisy or structurally complex settings. We propose SP-SPCA, a method that introduces a single equilibrium parameter into the regularization framework to adaptively adjust variable penalties. This modification of the L2 penalty provides flexible control over the trade-off between sparsity and explained variance while maintaining computational efficiency. Simulation studies show that the proposed method consistently outperforms standard sparse principal component methods in identifying sparse loading patterns, filtering noise variables, and preserving cumulative variance, especially in high-dimensional and noisy settings. Empirical applications to crime and financial market data further demonstrate its practical utility. In real data analyses, the method selects fewer but more relevant variables, thereby reducing model complexity while maintaining explanatory power. Overall, the proposed approach offers a robust and efficient alternative for sparse modeling in complex high-dimensional data, with clear advantages in stability, feature selection, and interpretability

</details>


### [86] [Maximin Robust Bayesian Experimental Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14094)
*Hany Abdulsamad,Sahel Iqbal,Christian A. Naesseth,Takuo Matsubara,Adrien Corenflos*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种对抗性贝叶斯实验设计框架，通过最大-最小博弈处理模型误设问题，使用Sibson α-互信息作为鲁棒目标，并采用PAC-Bayes方法获得有限样本误差控制的严格下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯实验设计在模型误设情况下存在脆弱性问题，传统方法对模型假设的微小偏差敏感，需要开发鲁棒的设计框架来应对现实世界中的模型不确定性。

Method: 将实验设计问题形式化为实验者与对抗性自然之间的最大-最小博弈，引入信息论约束；使用Sibson α-互信息作为鲁棒目标函数，识别α-倾斜后验为鲁棒信念更新；采用PAC-Bayes框架搜索随机设计策略，获得具有有限样本误差控制的严格高概率下界。

Result: 该方法建立了Sibson α-互信息作为鲁棒目标，Rényi散度作为条件信息增益的适当度量；通过PAC-Bayes框架获得了对鲁棒期望信息增益的严格高概率下界，有效控制了嵌套蒙特卡洛估计器的偏差和方差问题。

Conclusion: 提出的对抗性贝叶斯实验设计框架成功解决了模型误设下的脆弱性问题，通过信息论约束的最大-最小博弈和PAC-Bayes方法，为实际应用提供了具有理论保证的鲁棒设计策略。

Abstract: We address the brittleness of Bayesian experimental design under model misspecification by formulating the problem as a max--min game between the experimenter and an adversarial nature subject to information-theoretic constraints. We demonstrate that this approach yields a robust objective governed by Sibson's $α$-mutual information~(MI), which identifies the $α$-tilted posterior as the robust belief update and establishes the Rényi divergence as the appropriate measure of conditional information gain. To mitigate the bias and variance of nested Monte Carlo estimators needed to estimate Sibson's $α$-MI, we adopt a PAC-Bayes framework to search over stochastic design policies, yielding rigorous high-probability lower bounds on the robust expected information gain that explicitly control finite-sample error.

</details>


### [87] [AR-Flow VAE: A Structured Autoregressive Flow Prior Variational Autoencoder for Unsupervised Blind Source Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14441)
*Yuan-Hao Wei,Fu-Hao Deng,Lin-Yong Cui,Yan-Jie Sun*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: AR-Flow VAE：一种基于变分自编码器的盲源分离框架，通过自回归流先验增强对复杂非高斯行为和时序相关性的建模能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统盲源分离方法在建模复杂非高斯行为和结构化依赖（如时序相关性）方面存在局限，需要更灵活的潜在源建模方法

Method: 提出AR-Flow VAE框架，为每个潜在源配备参数自适应自回归流先验，增强建模灵活性；通过结构化先验设计为不同潜在维度分配不同先验，促进源信号分离

Result: 实验验证了该架构在盲源分离任务中的有效性，为未来研究AR-Flow VAE的可识别性和可解释性奠定了基础

Conclusion: AR-Flow VAE通过自回归流先验显著提升了潜在源建模的灵活性，能够有效处理复杂非高斯行为和结构化依赖，为盲源分离提供了新的解决方案

Abstract: Blind source separation (BSS) seeks to recover latent source signals from observed mixtures. Variational autoencoders (VAEs) offer a natural perspective for this problem: the latent variables can be interpreted as source components, the encoder can be viewed as a demixing mapping from observations to sources, and the decoder can be regarded as a remixing process from inferred sources back to observations. In this work, we propose AR-Flow VAE, a novel VAE-based framework for BSS in which each latent source is endowed with a parameter-adaptive autoregressive flow prior. This prior significantly enhances the flexibility of latent source modeling, enabling the framework to capture complex non-Gaussian behaviors and structured dependencies, such as temporal correlations, that are difficult to represent with conventional priors. In addition, the structured prior design assigns distinct priors to different latent dimensions, thereby encouraging the latent components to separate into different source signals under heterogeneous prior constraints. Experimental results validate the effectiveness of the proposed architecture for blind source separation. More importantly, this work provides a foundation for future investigations into the identifiability and interpretability of AR-Flow VAE.

</details>


### [88] [Convergence of Two Time-Scale Stochastic Approximation: A Martingale Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14481)
*Mathukumalli Vidyasagar*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文使用鞅方法分析两时间尺度随机逼近算法，建立了几乎必然收敛性，改进了收敛速率，并放宽了误差假设条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TTSSA文献大多假设方程为线性，且通常建立分布收敛或均值收敛，而非几乎必然收敛。此外，先前研究通常假设误差形成具有一致有界条件方差的鞅差序列，限制了算法的实际应用范围。

Method: 采用鞅方法分析TTSSA算法，该方法能够处理非线性方程，并允许误差具有非零条件均值和/或无界条件方差。建立了快慢子系统不同的收敛速率。

Result: 当测量误差具有零条件均值且条件方差有界时，迭代的均方误差以o(t^{-η})速率收敛到零（η∈(0,1)），优于Doan(2023)的O(t^{-2/3})结果，接近Doan(2024)对Polyak-Ruppert平均版本的O(t^{-1})速率。对于误差具有非零条件均值和/或无界条件方差的情况也建立了收敛速率。

Conclusion: 本文的鞅方法为TTSSA算法提供了更一般的收敛理论框架，放宽了误差假设，建立了几乎必然收敛性，并改进了收敛速率，特别是在非线性方程和更一般的误差条件下。

Abstract: In this paper, we analyze the two time-scale stochastic approximation (TTSSA) algorithm introduced in Borkar (1997) using a martingale approach. This approach leads to simple sufficient conditions for the iterations to be bounded almost surely, as well as estimates on the rate of convergence of the mean-squared error of the TTSSA algorithm to zero.
  Our theory is applicable to nonlinear equations, in contrast to many papers in the TTSSA literature which assume that the equations are linear. The convergence of TTSSA is proved in the "almost sure" sense, in contrast to earlier papers on TTSSA that establish convergence in distribution, convergence in the mean, and the like. Moreover, in this paper we establish different rates of convergence for the fast and the slow subsystems, perhaps for the first time. Finally, all of the above results to continue to hold in the case where the two measurement errors have nonzero conditional mean, and/or have conditional variances that grow without bound as the iterations proceed. This is in contrast to previous papers which assumed that the errors form a martingale difference sequence with uniformly bounded conditional variance.
  It is shown that when the measurement errors have zero conditional mean and the conditional variance remains bounded, the mean-squared error of the iterations converges to zero at a rate of $o(t^{-η})$ for all $η\in (0,1)$. This improves upon the rate of $O(t^{-2/3})$ proved in Doan (2023) (which is the best bound available to date). Our bound is virtually the same as the rate of $O(t^{-1})$ proved in Doan (2024), but for a Polyak-Ruppert averaged version of TTSSA, and not directly. Rates of convergence are also established for the case where the errors have nonzero conditional mean and/or unbounded conditional variance.

</details>


### [89] [Preconditioned One-Step Generative Modeling for Bayesian Inverse Problems in Function Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14798)
*Zilan Cheng,Li-Lian Wang,Zhongjian Wang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出基于一步生成式传输的函数空间贝叶斯逆问题机器学习算法，使用神经算子骨干学习完全条件化摊销采样器，解决白噪声参考在函数空间极限中的不稳定性问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MCMC方法在贝叶斯逆问题中需要重复求解PDE，计算成本高；现有基于白噪声参考的生成式方法在函数空间极限下不稳定

Method: 基于Mean Flows框架，使用神经算子骨干构建完全条件化摊销采样器，将参考高斯噪声映射为近似后验样本；采用先验对齐的各向异性高斯参考分布，确保传输的Lipschitz正则性

Result: 方法无需MCMC蒸馏，仅使用先验样本和模拟的部分噪声观测进行训练；训练后可在约10^-3秒内生成64×64后验样本，避免了MCMC的重复PDE求解，同时匹配关键后验统计量

Conclusion: 提出的方法成功解决了函数空间贝叶斯逆问题中白噪声参考的不稳定性问题，实现了高效的后验采样，在保持统计准确性的同时显著提升了计算效率

Abstract: We propose a machine-learning algorithm for Bayesian inverse problems in the function-space regime based on one-step generative transport. Building on the Mean Flows, we learn a fully conditional amortized sampler with a neural-operator backbone that maps a reference Gaussian noise to approximate posterior samples. We show that while white-noise references may be admissible at fixed discretization, they become incompatible with the function-space limit, leading to instability in inference for Bayesian problems arising from PDEs. To address this issue, we adopt a prior-aligned anisotropic Gaussian reference distribution and establish the Lipschitz regularity of the resulting transport. Our method is not distilled from MCMC: training relies only on prior samples and simulated partial and noisy observations. Once trained, it generates a $64\times64$ posterior sample in $\sim 10^{-3}$s, avoiding the repeated PDE solves of MCMC while matching key posterior summaries.

</details>


### [90] [Analyzing Error Sources in Global Feature Effect Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15057)
*Timo Heiß,Coco Bögel,Bernd Bischl,Giuseppe Casalicchio*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文系统分析了PD和ALE等全局特征效应估计中的误差来源，比较了使用训练数据与验证数据的影响，发现虽然理论上使用验证数据更干净，但实践中训练数据的偏差可忽略不计，而样本量影响更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局特征效应（如PD和ALE图）被广泛用于解释黑盒模型，但它们只是真实效应的估计，其可靠性受多种误差源影响。尽管这些方法很流行，但误差源尚未得到充分研究。特别是，使用训练数据还是验证数据来估计特征效应这一实际问题仍未解决。

Method: 1. 提供系统化的估计器层面分析，解构PD和ALE的偏差和方差来源；2. 推导均方误差分解，分离模型偏差、估计偏差、模型方差和估计方差；3. 分析这些误差分量对模型特性、数据选择和样本量的依赖性；4. 通过广泛的模拟研究验证理论发现，涵盖多种数据生成过程、学习器、估计策略（训练数据、验证数据、交叉验证）和样本量。

Result: 1. 虽然理论上使用验证数据是最干净的，但训练数据引起的潜在偏差在经验上可忽略不计，且被通常更大的样本量影响所主导；2. 估计方差取决于交互作用的存在和样本量，ALE对样本量特别敏感；3. 基于交叉验证的估计是有前景的方法，能减少模型方差分量，特别是对于过拟合模型。

Conclusion: 该分析为特征效应估计中的误差源提供了原则性解释，并为解释机器学习模型时选择估计策略提供了具体指导。研究揭示了在特征效应估计中，样本量通常比数据选择（训练vs验证）更重要，而交叉验证能有效减少模型方差。

Abstract: Global feature effects such as PD and ALE plots are widely used to interpret black-box models. However, they are only estimates of true underlying effects, and their reliability depends on multiple sources of error. Despite the popularity of global feature effects, these error sources are largely unexplored. In particular, the practically relevant question of whether to use training or holdout data to estimate feature effects remains unanswered. We address this gap by providing a systematic, estimator-level analysis that disentangles sources of bias and variance for PD and ALE. To this end, we derive a mean-squared-error decomposition that separates model bias, estimation bias, model variance, and estimation variance, and analyze their dependence on model characteristics, data selection, and sample size. We validate our theoretical findings through an extensive simulation study across multiple data-generating processes, learners, estimation strategies (training data, validation data, and cross-validation), and sample sizes. Our results reveal that, while using holdout data is theoretically the cleanest, potential biases arising from the training data are empirically negligible and dominated by the impact of the usually higher sample size. The estimation variance depends on both the presence of interactions and the sample size, with ALE being particularly sensitive to the latter. Cross-validation-based estimation is a promising approach that reduces the model variance component, particularly for overfitting models. Our analysis provides a principled explanation of the sources of error in feature effect estimates and offers concrete guidance on choosing estimation strategies when interpreting machine learning models.

</details>


### [91] [The Sampling Complexity of Condorcet Winner Identification in Dueling Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15189)
*El Mehdi Saad,Victor Thuot,Nicolas Verzelen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种新的随机决斗赌博机中Condorcet赢家识别方法，利用完整的差距矩阵而非仅与赢家的差距，获得改进的样本复杂度保证和新的下界分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Condorcet赢家识别方法通常只利用赢家与其他臂之间的差距信息，忽略了臂间比较的完整差距矩阵中的额外信息，限制了样本效率的提升。

Method: 引入新的识别程序，利用完整的差距矩阵Δ_{i,j}=q_{i,j}-1/2（其中q_{i,j}是臂i击败臂j的概率），而非仅利用Condorcet赢家与其他臂的差距。该方法通过挖掘赢家之外的信息性比较来提高效率。

Result: 获得了高概率、实例依赖的样本复杂度保证，在忽略对数因子情况下改进了已知最佳结果。同时提出了首个随机决斗赌博机中Condorcet赢家识别的下界，分析揭示了定位和估计差距矩阵中信息性条目的内在成本。

Conclusion: 新方法通过利用完整的差距矩阵信息，揭示了样本复杂度的新机制和权衡，这些在仅基于期望预算的渐近分析中未被捕捉，证明了方法的渐近最优性。

Abstract: We study best-arm identification in stochastic dueling bandits under the sole assumption that a Condorcet winner exists, i.e., an arm that wins each noisy pairwise comparison with probability at least $1/2$. We introduce a new identification procedure that exploits the full gap matrix $Δ_{i,j}=q_{i,j}-\tfrac12$ (where $q_{i,j}$ is the probability that arm $i$ beats arm $j$), rather than only the gaps between the Condorcet winner and the other arms. We derive high-probability, instance-dependent sample-complexity guarantees that (up to logarithmic factors) improve the best known ones by leveraging informative comparisons beyond those involving the winner. We complement these results with new lower bounds which, to our knowledge, are the first for Condorcet-winner identification in stochastic dueling bandits. Our lower-bound analysis isolates the intrinsic cost of locating informative entries in the gap matrix and estimating them to the required confidence, establishing the optimality of our non-asymptotic bounds. Overall, our results reveal new regimes and trade-offs in the sample complexity that are not captured by asymptotic analyses based only on the expected budget.

</details>


### [92] [Active Seriation: Efficient Ordering Recovery with Statistical Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15336)
*James Cheshire,Yann Issartel*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种主动排序算法，通过自适应查询成对相似度来恢复未知的项目排序，在噪声观测下能高概率恢复潜在排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动排序问题旨在通过自适应查询成对相似度来恢复未知的项目排序。观测数据是底层n×n置换罗宾逊矩阵的噪声测量，其置换编码了潜在排序。该框架允许算法从潜在排序的部分信息开始，包括从零开始的排序作为特例。

Method: 提出了一种主动排序算法，通过自适应查询策略来收集成对相似度观测。算法利用罗宾逊矩阵的结构特性，在噪声环境下逐步推断项目间的排序关系。算法设计考虑了从部分信息开始的情况，包括完全未知排序的特例。

Result: 在相似度矩阵满足均匀分离条件下，算法能以高概率恢复潜在排序。建立了最优性能保证，包括错误概率和成功恢复所需观测数量的理论界限。

Conclusion: 提出的主动排序算法在理论保证下能有效解决从噪声观测中恢复项目排序的问题，为主动排序问题提供了最优性能保证。

Abstract: Active seriation aims at recovering an unknown ordering of $n$ items by adaptively querying pairwise similarities. The observations are noisy measurements of entries of an underlying $n$ x $n$ permuted Robinson matrix, whose permutation encodes the latent ordering. The framework allows the algorithm to start with partial information on the latent ordering, including seriation from scratch as a special case. We propose an active seriation algorithm that provably recovers the latent ordering with high probability. Under a uniform separation condition on the similarity matrix, optimal performance guarantees are established, both in terms of the probability of error and the number of observations required for successful recovery.

</details>


### [93] [Persistence Spheres: a Bi-continuous Linear Representation of Measures for Partial Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15384)
*Matteo Pegoraro*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 改进和扩展持久性球面表示，这是一种将持久性图等可积测度映射到球面连续函数的稳定表示方法，在拓扑机器学习中具有连续逆映射特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拓扑机器学习中的表示方法缺乏在图像上具有连续逆映射的显式表示。持久性球面旨在提供一种参数自由的稳定表示，能够更好地匹配部分传输距离的删除机制，并避免临时重加权。

Method: 基于凸几何构建：对于正测度，定义ReLU积分作为提升zonoid的支撑函数。通过有符号对角线增强来编码部分传输，改进定义以更好地匹配POT₁删除机制。将可积测度μ映射到球面连续函数S(μ)∈C(𝕊²)。

Result: 持久性球面表示在聚类、回归和分类任务中具有竞争力，通常优于持久性图像、持久性景观、持久性样条和切片Wasserstein核基线。S(0)和S(μ)之间的均匀范数仅取决于μ的持久性，无需临时重加权。

Conclusion: 持久性球面提供了一种参数自由、稳定的拓扑数据表示方法，在多种数据类型和应用任务中表现优异，是拓扑机器学习中首个具有连续逆映射特性的显式表示。

Abstract: We improve and extend persistence spheres, introduced in~\cite{pegoraro2025persistence}. Persistence spheres map an integrable measure $μ$ on the upper half-plane, including persistence diagrams (PDs) as counting measures, to a function $S(μ)\in C(\mathbb{S}^2)$, and the map is stable with respect to 1-Wasserstein partial transport distance $\mathrm{POT}_1$. Moreover, to the best of our knowledge, persistence spheres are the first explicit representation used in topological machine learning for which continuity of the inverse on the image is established at every compactly supported target. Recent bounded-cardinality bi-Lipschitz embedding results in partial transport spaces, despite being powerful, are not given by the kind of explicit summary map considered here. Our construction is rooted in convex geometry: for positive measures, the defining ReLU integral is the support function of the lift zonoid. Building on~\cite{pegoraro2025persistence}, we refine the definition to better match the $\mathrm{POT}_1$ deletion mechanism, encoding partial transport via a signed diagonal augmentation. In particular, for integrable $μ$, the uniform norm between $S(0)$ and $S(μ)$ depends only on the persistence of $μ$, without any need of ad-hoc re-weightings, reflecting optimal transport to the diagonal at persistence cost. This yields a parameter-free representation at the level of measures (up to numerical discretization), while accommodating future extensions where $μ$ is a smoothed measure derived from PDs (e.g., persistence intensity functions~\citep{wu2024estimation}). Across clustering, regression, and classification tasks involving functional data, time series, graphs, meshes, and point clouds, the updated persistence spheres are competitive and often improve upon persistence images, persistence landscapes, persistence splines, and sliced Wasserstein kernel baselines.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [94] [Translational Gaps in Graph Transformers for Longitudinal EHR Prediction: A Critical Appraisal of GT-BEHRT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13231)
*Krish Tadigotla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 对GT-BEHRT图-Transformer架构的批判性综述，分析其在电子健康记录预测中的性能增益是否真实反映架构优势，并评估其临床部署可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Transformer模型将临床就诊视为无序代码集合，无法捕捉就诊内的有意义关系。图-Transformer方法旨在建模就诊级结构，同时保留学习长期时序模式的能力，但需要评估其实际效益。

Method: 对GT-BEHRT架构进行七维度分析：表示设计、预训练策略、队列构建透明度、超越区分的评估、公平性评估、可重复性和部署可行性。在MIMIC-IV重症监护结果和All of Us研究计划的心力衰竭预测中进行评估。

Result: GT-BEHRT在365天内心力衰竭预测中表现出强区分能力（AUROC 94.37±0.20，AUPRC 73.96±0.83，F1 64.70±0.85），但存在校准分析缺失、公平性评估不完整、对队列选择敏感、跨表型和预测时间范围分析有限等缺陷。

Conclusion: GT-BEHRT代表了EHR表示学习的有意义架构进步，但在可靠支持临床决策前，需要更严格的评估，重点关注校准、公平性和部署可行性。

Abstract: Transformer-based models have improved predictive modeling on longitudinal electronic health records through large-scale self-supervised pretraining. However, most EHR transformer architectures treat each clinical encounter as an unordered collection of codes, which limits their ability to capture meaningful relationships within a visit. Graph-transformer approaches aim to address this limitation by modeling visit-level structure while retaining the ability to learn long-term temporal patterns. This paper provides a critical review of GT-BEHRT, a graph-transformer architecture evaluated on MIMIC-IV intensive care outcomes and heart failure prediction in the All of Us Research Program. We examine whether the reported performance gains reflect genuine architectural benefits and whether the evaluation methodology supports claims of robustness and clinical relevance. We analyze GT-BEHRT across seven dimensions relevant to modern machine learning systems, including representation design, pretraining strategy, cohort construction transparency, evaluation beyond discrimination, fairness assessment, reproducibility, and deployment feasibility. GT-BEHRT reports strong discrimination for heart failure prediction within 365 days, with AUROC 94.37 +/- 0.20, AUPRC 73.96 +/- 0.83, and F1 64.70 +/- 0.85. Despite these results, we identify several important gaps, including the lack of calibration analysis, incomplete fairness evaluation, sensitivity to cohort selection, limited analysis across phenotypes and prediction horizons, and limited discussion of practical deployment considerations. Overall, GT-BEHRT represents a meaningful architectural advance in EHR representation learning, but more rigorous evaluation focused on calibration, fairness, and deployment is needed before such models can reliably support clinical decision-making.

</details>


### [95] [RFX-Fuse: Breiman and Cutler's Unified ML Engine + Native Explainable Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13234)
*Chris Kuchar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RFX-Fuse实现Breiman和Cutler原始随机森林的完整愿景，提供分类、回归、无监督学习、相似性度量、异常检测、缺失值插补和可视化等一体化功能，替代现代ML管道中需要5+个独立工具的场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习管道需要多个独立工具（XGBoost、FAISS、SHAP、Isolation Forest等）来完成不同任务，而Breiman和Cutler的原始随机森林设计本应是一个统一的ML引擎，包含多种功能，但现代库如scikit-learn从未实现这些完整功能。

Method: RFX-Fuse（随机森林X压缩-森林统一学习和相似性引擎）提供原生GPU/CPU支持，使用单一组树结构一次训练，实现多种功能。创新贡献包括：(1) 邻近重要性-原生可解释相似性：邻近度量样本相似性，邻近重要性解释原因；(2) 数据集特定的插补验证-通过评估插补数据看起来有多真实来对插补方法进行排序，无需真实标签。

Result: RFX-Fuse提供了一个1到2个模型对象的替代方案，用单一组树结构替代现代ML管道中需要5+个独立工具的场景，实现了Breiman和Cutler原始随机森林的完整愿景。

Conclusion: RFX-Fuse成功实现了随机森林作为统一机器学习引擎的原始设计理念，通过单一模型对象提供多种功能，简化了现代机器学习工作流程，并引入了邻近重要性和数据集特定插补验证等创新功能。

Abstract: Breiman and Cutler's original Random Forest was designed as a unified ML engine -- not merely an ensemble predictor. Their implementation included classification, regression, unsupervised learning, proximity-based similarity, outlier
  detection, missing value imputation, and visualization -- capabilities that modern libraries like scikit-learn never implemented. RFX-Fuse (Random Forests X [X=compression] -- Forest Unified Learning and Similarity Engine) delivers
  Breiman and Cutler's complete vision with native GPU/CPU support. Modern ML pipelines require 5+ separate tools -- XGBoost for prediction, FAISS for similarity, SHAP for explanations, Isolation Forest for outliers, custom code for
  importance. RFX-Fuse provides a 1 to 2 model object alternative -- a single set of trees grown once. Novel Contributions: (1) Proximity Importance -- native explainable similarity: proximity measures that samples are similar;
  proximity importance explains why. (2) Dataset-specific imputation validation for general tabular data -- ranking imputation methods by how real the imputed data looks, without ground truth labels.

</details>


### [96] [Continual Fine-Tuning with Provably Accurate and Parameter-Free Task Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13235)
*Hang Thi-Thuy Le,Long Minh Bui,Minh Hoang,Trong Nghia Hoang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种结合输入适应和参数适应优势的持续微调方法，通过参数自由检索实现测试时自适应表示使用


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续微调方法分为输入适应和参数适应两类：输入适应方法依赖检索相关提示但容易遗忘；参数适应方法使用固定输入嵌入避免遗忘但牺牲表示适应性。需要结合两者的优势

Method: 提出参数适应方法，包含两个关键组件：(1)自适应模块组合策略，学习信息丰富的任务特定更新以保留和补充先验知识；(2)基于聚类的检索机制，捕获每个任务的独特表示特征，实现测试时自适应表示使用

Result: 通过理论分析推导任务检索误差界，证明良好组织的聚类结构能够实现可靠检索。实验表明这些组件协同工作，在任务语义大幅变化时显著提升检索和预测性能

Conclusion: 该方法成功结合了输入适应和参数适应的优势，通过参数自由检索实现自适应表示使用，在保持先前任务性能的同时适应新任务，为持续学习提供了新思路

Abstract: Continual fine-tuning aims to adapt a pre-trained backbone to new tasks sequentially while preserving performance on earlier tasks whose data are no longer available. Existing approaches fall into two categories which include input- and parameter-adaptation. Input-adaptation methods rely on retrieving the most relevant prompts at test time, but require continuously learning a retrieval function that is prone to forgetting. Parameter-adaptation methods instead use a fixed input embedding function to enable retrieval-free prediction and avoid forgetting, but sacrifice representation adaptability. To combine their best strengths, we propose a new parameter-adaptation method that enables adaptive use of input embeddings during test time with parameter-free retrieval. We derive task-retrieval error bounds for a clustering-based, parameter-free paradigm, providing theoretical guarantees that link low retrieval error to structural properties of task-specific representation clusters, revealing a fresh insight into how well-organized clustering structure will enable reliable retrieval. Motivated by this insight, our method is designed with two key components: (i) an adaptive module composition strategy that learns informative task-specific updates to preserve and complement prior knowledge, and (ii) a clustering-based retrieval mechanism that captures distinct representation signatures for each task, enabling adaptive representation use at test time. Extensive experiments show that these components work synergistically to improve retrieval and predictive performance under large shifts in task semantics.

</details>


### [97] [Introducing Feature-Based Trajectory Clustering, a clustering algorithm for longitudinal data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13254)
*Marie-Pierre Sylvestre,Laurence Boulanger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种新的纵向数据聚类算法，通过数学公式将个体映射到欧几里得空间，再使用谱聚类识别具有相似时间演化特征的个体群组


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向数据中个体时间演化变量存在差异，但可能存在许多个体共享的共性特征模式，需要开发能够识别这些共享特征模式的聚类方法

Method: 采用两步法：1) 使用特定数学公式将每个个体映射到欧几里得空间，坐标捕捉各种特征特征；2) 对生成的点云应用谱聚类算法寻找聚类

Result: 提出了一种新的纵向数据聚类框架，能够识别共享相似时间演化特征的个体群组

Conclusion: 该方法为纵向数据分析提供了一种有效的聚类方法，能够发现个体间共享的时间演化模式，有助于理解群体行为模式

Abstract: We present a new algorithm for clustering longitudinal data. Data of this type can be conceptualized as consisting of individuals and, for each such individual, observations of a time-dependent variable made at various times. Generically, the specific way in which this variable evolves with time is different from one individual to the next. However, there may also be commonalities; specific characteristic features of the time evolution shared by many individuals. The purpose of the method we put forward is to find clusters of individual whose underlying time-dependent variables share such characteristic features. This is done in two steps. The first step identifies each individual to a point in Euclidean space whose coordinates are determined by specific mathematical formulae meant to capture a variety of characteristic features. The second step finds the clusters by applying the Spectral Clustering algorithm to the resulting point cloud.

</details>


### [98] [Beyond Attention: True Adaptive World Models via Spherical Kernel Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13263)
*Vladimer Khasia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出球形核算子(SKO)框架替代标准注意力机制，通过将数据流形投影到统一超球面并使用超球面多项式进行积分重构，避免传统世界模型的数学缺陷


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于参数化潜在空间的世界模型存在数学缺陷：仅将流形学习问题转移到潜在空间，当数据分布变化时，潜在流形随之变化，迫使预测算子隐式重新学习新的拓扑结构。此外，点积注意力等正算子存在饱和现象，限制预测能力并受维度诅咒影响。

Method: 引入球形核算子(SKO)框架，将未知数据流形投影到统一环境超球面上，使用局部化的超球面(Gegenbauer)多项式序列，通过直接积分重构目标函数。该局部球形多项式核不是严格正定的，因此避免了饱和现象。

Result: SKO的近似误差界限严格依赖于内在流形维度q，而非环境维度。通过将其非归一化输出形式化为真实测度支持估计器，SKO在数学上将真实环境转移动态与智能体的有偏观测频率解耦。实证评估表明SKO显著加速收敛并在自回归语言建模中优于标准注意力基线。

Conclusion: SKO提供了一个数学严谨的世界模型构建范式，通过重新定义核心预测机制解决了传统方法的根本缺陷，为世界模型的发展提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: The pursuit of world model based artificial intelligence has predominantly relied on projecting high-dimensional observations into parameterized latent spaces, wherein transition dynamics are subsequently learned. However, this conventional paradigm is mathematically flawed: it merely displaces the manifold learning problem into the latent space. When the underlying data distribution shifts, the latent manifold shifts accordingly, forcing the predictive operator to implicitly relearn the new topological structure. Furthermore, by classical approximation theory, positive operators like dot product attention inevitably suffer from the saturation phenomenon, permanently bottlenecking their predictive capacity and leaving them vulnerable to the curse of dimensionality. In this paper, we formulate a mathematically rigorous paradigm for world model construction by redefining the core predictive mechanism. Inspired by Ryan O'Dowd's foundational work we introduce Spherical Kernel Operator (SKO), a framework that replaces standard attention. By projecting the unknown data manifold onto a unified ambient hypersphere and utilizing a localized sequence of ultraspherical (Gegenbauer) polynomials, SKO performs direct integral reconstruction of the target function. Because this localized spherical polynomial kernel is not strictly positive, it bypasses the saturation phenomenon, yielding approximation error bounds that depend strictly on the intrinsic manifold dimension q, rather than the ambient dimension. Furthermore, by formalizing its unnormalized output as an authentic measure support estimator, SKO mathematically decouples the true environmental transition dynamics from the biased observation frequency of the agent. Empirical evaluations confirm that SKO significantly accelerates convergence and outperforms standard attention baselines in autoregressive language modeling.

</details>


### [99] [Federated Personal Knowledge Graph Completion with Lightweight Large Language Models for Personalized Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13264)
*Fernando Spadea,Oshani Seneviratne*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedTREK-LM框架结合轻量级LLM、演化个人知识图谱、联邦学习和Kahneman-Tversky优化，实现去中心化个性化推荐，在电影和食谱推荐任务上性能提升4倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化推荐日益依赖用户隐私数据，需要能够在无需集中用户信息的情况下适应个体需求的解决方案，推动去中心化个性化方法的发展。

Method: 提出FedTREK-LM框架，整合轻量级大语言模型(Qwen3系列)、演化个人知识图谱、联邦学习和Kahneman-Tversky优化，通过结构化PKG提示LLM进行上下文感知推理，实现个性化推荐。

Result: 在三个轻量级Qwen3模型(0.6B, 1.7B, 4B)上，FedTREK-LM显著优于最先进的KG补全和联邦推荐基线(HAKE, KBGAT, FedKGRec)，在电影和食物基准测试中F1分数提升超过4倍。真实用户数据对个性化效果至关重要，合成数据会使性能下降高达46%。

Conclusion: FedTREK-LM为自适应、LLM驱动的个性化提供了一个实用范式，能够泛化到去中心化、演化的用户个人知识图谱场景中。

Abstract: Personalized recommendation increasingly relies on private user data, motivating approaches that can adapt to individuals without centralizing their information. We present Federated Targeted Recommendations with Evolving Knowledge graphs and Language Models (FedTREK-LM), a framework that unifies lightweight large language models (LLMs), evolving personal knowledge graphs (PKGs), federated learning (FL), and Kahneman-Tversky Optimization to enable scalable, decentralized personalization. By prompting LLMs with structured PKGs, FedTREK-LM performs context-aware reasoning for personalized recommendation tasks such as movie and recipe suggestions. Across three lightweight Qwen3 models (0.6B, 1.7B, 4B), FedTREK-LM consistently and substantially outperforms state-of-the-art KG completion and federated recommendation baselines (HAKE, KBGAT, and FedKGRec), achieving more than a 4x improvement in F1-score on the movie and food benchmarks. Our results further show that real user data is critical for effective personalization, as synthetic data degrades performance by up to 46%. Overall, FedTREK-LM offers a practical paradigm for adaptive, LLM-powered personalization that generalizes across decentralized, evolving user PKGs.

</details>


### [100] [Knowledge, Rules and Their Embeddings: Two Paths towards Neuro-Symbolic JEPA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13265)
*Yongchao Huang,Hassan Raza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RiJEPA双向神经符号框架，通过能量约束和多模态双编码器将结构化归纳偏置注入JEPA训练，同时将离散符号规则松弛为连续可微逻辑，实现连续规则发现和梯度引导推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代自监督预测架构擅长捕捉高维数据的复杂统计相关性，但缺乏内化可验证人类逻辑的机制，容易受到虚假相关性和捷径学习的影响。传统基于规则的推理系统提供严谨、可解释的逻辑，但存在离散边界和NP难组合爆炸问题。需要弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: 提出Rule-informed Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (RiJEPA)双向神经符号框架：1) 通过能量约束(EBC)和多模态双编码器将结构化归纳偏置注入JEPA训练，重塑表示流形；2) 将刚性离散符号规则松弛为连续可微逻辑，利用梯度引导的Langevin扩散在规则能量景观中进行连续规则发现。

Result: 在合成拓扑模拟和高风险临床用例上的实证评估证实了方法的有效性。该框架支持无条件联合生成、条件前向和溯因推理、边缘预测翻译等新范式。

Conclusion: RiJEPA框架为稳健、生成式和可解释的神经符号表示学习建立了强大基础，通过双向整合统计学习和符号推理，克服了各自局限性。

Abstract: Modern self-supervised predictive architectures excel at capturing complex statistical correlations from high-dimensional data but lack mechanisms to internalize verifiable human logic, leaving them susceptible to spurious correlations and shortcut learning. Conversely, traditional rule-based inference systems offer rigorous, interpretable logic but suffer from discrete boundaries and NP-hard combinatorial explosion. To bridge this divide, we propose a bidirectional neuro-symbolic framework centered around Rule-informed Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (RiJEPA). In the first direction, we inject structured inductive biases into JEPA training via Energy-Based Constraints (EBC) and a multi-modal dual-encoder architecture. This fundamentally reshapes the representation manifold, replacing arbitrary statistical correlations with geometrically sound logical basins. In the second direction, we demonstrate that by relaxing rigid, discrete symbolic rules into a continuous, differentiable logic, we can bypass traditional combinatorial search for new rule generation. By leveraging gradient-guided Langevin diffusion within the rule energy landscape, we introduce novel paradigms for continuous rule discovery, which enable unconditional joint generation, conditional forward and abductive inference, and marginal predictive translation. Empirical evaluations on both synthetic topological simulations and a high-stakes clinical use case confirm the efficacy of our approach. Ultimately, this framework establishes a powerful foundation for robust, generative, and interpretable neuro-symbolic representation learning.

</details>


### [101] [CAMEL-CLIP: Channel-aware Multimodal Electroencephalography-text Alignment for Generalizable Brain Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13272)
*Hanseul Choi,Jinyeong Park,Seongwon Jin,Sungho Park,Jibum Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAMEL-CLIP是一个针对EEG信号的通道感知多模态基础模型，通过通道属性位置编码、动态通道投影和双级对比学习，实现对异构通道配置的鲁棒性，并在下游任务中取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG基础模型对通道异质性（如通道组成或顺序变化）敏感，限制了其泛化能力和实际应用。需要开发对异构通道配置鲁棒且适用于多样化下游任务的EEG基础模型。

Method: 提出CAMEL-CLIP模型，包含三个核心组件：1) 通道属性位置编码，通过语义信息识别通道；2) 动态通道投影，独立投影每个通道而不压缩特征；3) 双级对比学习，联合执行通道级和样本级对比学习，捕获通道特定和全局信号特征。

Result: 实验结果表明，CAMEL-CLIP在线性探测下达到最先进性能，并且优于依赖完全微调的现有基础模型。

Conclusion: CAMEL-CLIP通过创新的通道感知设计，成功解决了EEG基础模型对通道异质性的敏感问题，为EEG信号处理提供了鲁棒且通用的多模态表示学习框架。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) foundation models have shown promise for learning generalizable representations, yet they remain sensitive to channel heterogeneity, such as changes in channel composition or ordering. We propose channel-aware multimodal EEG-text alignment contrastive language-image pretraining (CAMEL-CLIP), a contrastive EEG-text multimodal foundation model designed to be robust to heterogeneous channel configurations and widely applicable to diverse downstream tasks. CAMEL-CLIP introduces three key components: (1) channel attribute-based positional encoding, which identifies channels through semantic information; (2) dynamic channel projection, which generates variable-length embeddings by independently projecting each channel without feature compression; and (3) dual-level contrastive learning, which jointly performs channel-level and sample-level contrastive learning to capture both channel-specific and global signal characteristics. Experimental results demonstrate that CAMEL-CLIP achieves state-of-the-art performance under linear-probing and outperforms existing foundation models that rely on full-finetuning.

</details>


### [102] [Learning from Partial Chain-of-Thought via Truncated-Reasoning Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13274)
*Gianluigi Silvestri,Edoardo Cetin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRSD是一种轻量级后训练方法，通过自蒸馏让模型从部分推理轨迹中获得正确答案，减少推理计算成本


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向推理的语言模型通过生成长思维链获得强性能，但带来过高计算成本，存在冗余或低效推理问题

Method: 提出截断推理自蒸馏（TRSD）：冻结教师模型生成完整推理轨迹和答案分布，学生模型仅基于截断的推理前缀训练以匹配教师答案分布

Result: TRSD在多个推理基准和token预算下提升截断推理鲁棒性，显著减少准确率损失；TRSD训练模型即使不截断也自然输出更短推理轨迹，大幅降低推理成本

Conclusion: TRSD是有效的轻量级后训练方法，能显著减少推理计算成本同时保持模型性能，为高效推理提供新途径

Abstract: Reasoning-oriented language models achieve strong performance by generating long chain-of-thought traces at inference time. However, this capability comes with substantial and often excessive computational cost, which can materialize in redundant or inefficient reasoning. We study this setting and introduce Truncated-Reasoning Self-Distillation (TRSD), a lightweight post-training procedure that encourages models to produce correct predictions from partial reasoning traces. In TRSD, a frozen teacher model first generates a full reasoning trace and evaluates the corresponding answer distribution conditioned on the prompt and the complete reasoning to construct a synthetic training target. A student model with the same architecture is then trained to match the teacher's answer distribution while being conditioned only on a truncated prefix of its reasoning trace. Across multiple reasoning benchmarks and token budgets, we demonstrate that TRSD improves robustness to truncated inference, with far reduced accuracy tradeoffs when applied to a diverse set of reasoning models. Moreover, although never explicitly regularized for shorter generation during training, we also find that TRSD-trained models inherently output shorter reasoning traces without truncation, significantly reducing inference-time costs even without artificial interventions.

</details>


### [103] [PREBA: Surgical Duration Prediction via PCA-Weighted Retrieval-Augmented LLMs and Bayesian Averaging Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13275)
*Wanyin Wu,Kanxue Li,Baosheng Yu,Haoyun Zhao,Yibing Zhan,Dapeng Tao,Hua Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PREBA是一个检索增强框架，通过PCA加权检索和贝叶斯平均聚合，将LLM预测基于机构特定的临床证据和统计先验，显著提升手术时长预测精度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术时长预测方法存在局限性：监督学习方法需要高质量标注数据和计算密集型训练；零样本LLM推理缺乏机构特定的临床上下文（如本地人口统计学和病例分布），导致预测临床不对齐且不稳定

Method: PREBA框架包含三个核心组件：1) 将异构临床特征编码到统一表示空间；2) PCA加权检索识别临床相关历史病例作为LLM的证据上下文；3) 贝叶斯平均聚合将多轮LLM预测与群体级统计先验融合，生成校准的预测

Result: 在两个真实临床数据集上使用三种SOTA LLM（Qwen3、DeepSeek-R1、HuatuoGPT-o1）评估，PREBA显著提升性能：MAE降低高达40%，R²从-0.13提升到0.62，达到与监督ML方法竞争的准确度

Conclusion: PREBA通过检索增强方法有效解决了零样本LLM推理在手术时长预测中的临床不对齐问题，提供了一种无需训练、基于证据的预测框架，在保持计算效率的同时实现了与监督方法相当的准确性

Abstract: Accurate prediction of surgical duration is pivotal for hospital resource management. Although recent supervised learning approaches-from machine learning (ML) to fine-tuned large language models (LLMs)-have shown strong performance, they remain constrained by the need for high-quality labeled data and computationally intensive training. In contrast, zero-shot LLM inference offers a promising training-free alternative but it lacks grounding in institution-specific clinical context (e.g., local demographics and case-mix distributions), making its predictions clinically misaligned and prone to instability. To address these limitations, we present PREBA, a retrieval-augmented framework that integrates PCA-weighted retrieval and Bayesian averaging aggregation to ground LLM predictions in institution-specific clinical evidence and statistical priors. The core of PREBA is to construct an evidence-based prompt for the LLM, comprising (1) the most clinically similar historical surgical cases and (2) clinical statistical priors. To achieve this, PREBA first encodes heterogeneous clinical features into a unified representation space enabling systematic retrieval. It then performs PCA-weighted retrieval to identify clinically relevant historical cases, which form the evidence context supplied to the LLM. Finally, PREBA applies Bayesian averaging to fuse multi-round LLM predictions with population-level statistical priors, yielding calibrated and clinically plausible duration estimates. We evaluate PREBA on two real-world clinical datasets using three state-of-the-art LLMs, including Qwen3, DeepSeek-R1, and HuatuoGPT-o1. PREBA significantly improves performance-for instance, reducing MAE by up to 40% and raising R^2 from -0.13 to 0.62 over zero-shot inference-and it achieves accuracy competitive with supervised ML methods, demonstrating strong effectiveness and generalization.

</details>


### [104] [FastODT: A tree-based framework for efficient continual learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13276)
*Daniel Bretsko,Piotr Walas,Devashish Khulbe,Sebastian Stros,Stanislav Sobolevsky,Tomas Satura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Hoeffding边界控制生长的遗忘树模型，用于非平稳环境下的在线学习，在能源和环境感知时间序列基准测试中表现优异，计算效率高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界部署的机器学习模型需要在数据分布不断演变和计算资源受限的环境中运行，特别是在能源时间序列、天气监测和环境感知等非平稳领域。模型需要具备适应性、持续学习和长期知识保留能力。

Method: 提出一种基于Hoeffding边界控制生长的遗忘树模型，无缝集成快速学习和推理、高效内存管理和鲁棒知识保留，支持在线学习。

Result: 在能源和环境感知时间序列基准测试上的广泛实验表明，该框架性能与现有在线和批量学习方法相当甚至超越，同时保持卓越的计算效率。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了适应性、持续更新和高效重训练的核心目标，无需完整模型重训练，为资源受限且需要持续适应的现实世界非平稳环境部署提供了可扩展和资源感知的基础。

Abstract: Machine learning models deployed in real-world settings must operate under evolving data distributions and constrained computational resources. This challenge is particularly acute in non-stationary domains such as energy time series, weather monitoring, and environmental sensing. To remain effective, models must support adaptability, continuous learning, and long-term knowledge retention. This paper introduces a oblivious tree-based model with Hoeffding bound controlling its growth. It seamlessly integrates rapid learning and inference with efficient memory management and robust knowledge preservation, thus allowing for online learning. Extensive experiments across energy and environmental sensing time-series benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves performance competitive with, and in several cases surpassing, existing online and batch learning methods, while maintaining superior computational efficiency. Collectively, these results demonstrate that the proposed approach fulfills the core objectives of adaptability, continual updating, and efficient retraining without full model retraining. The framework provides a scalable and resource-aware foundation for deployment in real-world non-stationary environments where resources are constrained and sustained adaptation is essential.

</details>


### [105] [Learning Retrieval Models with Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13277)
*Thibault Formal,Maxime Louis,Hervé Dejean,Stéphane Clinchant*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPLARE是一种基于稀疏自编码器的学习稀疏检索方法，在多语言和跨域检索任务中优于基于词汇表的传统方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏自编码器（SAEs）能够将大语言模型的密集表示分解为可解释的潜在特征，这为学习稀疏检索（LSR）提供了自然基础。现有LSR方法将输入序列投影到词汇空间，而基于SAE的表示有望产生更具语义结构、表达力更强且语言无关的特征。

Method: 提出SPLARE方法，基于稀疏自编码器训练LSR模型。利用最近发布的开源SAEs，将查询和文档编码为高维稀疏表示，这些表示针对高效检索进行了优化。

Result: SPLARE在多语言和跨域设置中持续优于基于词汇表的LSR方法。SPLARE-7B在MMTEB的多语言和英语检索任务中取得最佳结果，能够为广泛语言和领域生成可泛化的稀疏潜在嵌入。还开发了参数更少的2B参数变体。

Conclusion: 基于稀疏自编码器的学习稀疏检索方法比传统词汇表方法更具优势，特别是在多语言和跨域场景中。SPLARE展示了SAE-based LSR在生成语义结构化、表达力强且语言无关的稀疏表示方面的潜力。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) provide a powerful mechanism for decomposing the dense representations produced by Large Language Models (LLMs) into interpretable latent features. We posit that SAEs constitute a natural foundation for Learned Sparse Retrieval (LSR), whose objective is to encode queries and documents into high-dimensional sparse representations optimized for efficient retrieval. In contrast to existing LSR approaches that project input sequences into the vocabulary space, SAE-based representations offer the potential to produce more semantically structured, expressive, and language-agnostic features. Building on this insight, we introduce SPLARE, a method to train SAE-based LSR models. Our experiments, relying on recently released open-source SAEs, demonstrate that this technique consistently outperforms vocabulary-based LSR in multilingual and out-of-domain settings. SPLARE-7B, a multilingual retrieval model capable of producing generalizable sparse latent embeddings for a wide range of languages and domains, achieves top results on MMTEB's multilingual and English retrieval tasks. We also developed a 2B-parameter variant with a significantly lighter footprint.

</details>


### [106] [Demand Acceptance using Reinforcement Learning for Dynamic Vehicle Routing Problem with Emission Quota](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13279)
*Farid Najar,Dominique Barth,Yann Strozecki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出并形式化了DS-QVRP-RR问题，这是一个结合动态需求接受、路径规划与全局排放约束的新型车辆路径问题，采用双层优化框架和强化学习与组合优化相结合的混合算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统车辆路径问题通常忽略排放约束或将其作为次要目标，而实际物流运营中需要同时考虑动态需求接受、路径优化和严格的排放配额限制，现有方法难以有效处理这种多目标动态优化问题。

Method: 提出双层优化框架：上层处理需求拒绝决策和路径生成，下层进行具体路径优化；开发混合算法，将强化学习用于需求拒绝的预见性决策，结合组合优化技术进行路径规划。

Result: 通过全面的计算研究，与传统方法相比，所提方法在不同类型输入下都表现出优越性，即使在问题时间范围不确定的情况下也能有效工作。

Conclusion: DS-QVRP-RR问题具有实际应用价值，提出的双层优化框架和混合算法能够有效处理动态需求、排放约束和不确定时间范围的复杂车辆路径问题。

Abstract: This paper introduces and formalizes the Dynamic and Stochastic Vehicle Routing Problem with Emission Quota (DS-QVRP-RR), a novel routing problems that integrates dynamic demand acceptance and routing with a global emission constraint. A key contribution is a two-layer optimization framework designed to facilitate anticipatory rejections of demands and generation of new routes. To solve this, we develop hybrid algorithms that combine reinforcement learning with combinatorial optimization techniques. We present a comprehensive computational study that compares our approach against traditional methods. Our findings demonstrate the relevance of our approach for different types of inputs, even when the horizon of the problem is uncertain.

</details>


### [107] [A Stability-Aware Frozen Euler Autoencoder for Physics-Informed Tracking in Continuum Mechanics (SAFE-PIT-CM)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13280)
*Emil Hovad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAFE-PIT-CM是一种基于物理信息跟踪的稳定性感知冻结欧拉自编码器，用于从物理过程视频中恢复材料参数和时间场演化，通过冻结PDE算子和子步长策略解决稳定性问题，支持零样本推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从物理过程视频中恢复材料参数和场演化通常需要地面真实标签，且传统方法在处理大时间间隔帧时存在数值稳定性问题，导致参数估计不准确。

Method: 采用自编码器架构：卷积编码器将每帧映射到潜在场；SAFE算子（冻结PDE算子）通过子步长有限差分向前传播；解码器重建视频。通过注意力机制估计传输系数α，仅使用SAFE损失作为监督，无需地面真实标签。

Result: 在热方程（扩散，α<0）和反向热方程（迁移率，α>0）上验证有效。零样本推理模式（无训练数据）达到与预训练模型相当的精度。解决了大时间间隔下的数值不稳定问题，实现准确参数恢复。

Conclusion: SAFE-PIT-CM通过冻结PDE算子和子步长策略解决了物理信息跟踪中的稳定性问题，实现了无监督参数估计和零样本推理，具有可解释性且适用于任何可卷积有限差分离散的PDE。

Abstract: We introduce a Stability-Aware Frozen Euler autoencoder for Physics-Informed Tracking in Continuum Mechanics (SAFE-PIT-CM) that recovers material parameters and temporal field evolution from videos of physical processes. The architecture is an autoencoder whose latent-space transition is governed by a frozen PDE operator: a convolutional encoder maps each frame to a latent field; the SAFE operator propagates it forward via sub-stepped finite differences; and a decoder reconstructs the video. Because the physics is embedded as a frozen, differentiable layer, backpropagation yields gradients that directly supervise an attention-based estimator for the transport coefficient alpha, requiring no ground-truth labels. The SAFE operator is the central contribution. Temporal snapshots are saved at intervals far larger than the simulation time step; a forward Euler step at the frame interval violates the von Neumann stability condition, causing alpha to collapse to an unphysical value. The SAFE operator resolves this by sub-stepping the frozen finite-difference stencil to match the original temporal resolution, restoring stability and enabling accurate parameter recovery. We demonstrate SAFE-PIT-CM on the heat equation (diffusion, alpha < 0) and the reverse heat equation (mobility, alpha > 0). SAFE-PIT-CM also supports zero-shot inference: learning alpha from a single simulation with no training data, using only the SAFE loss as supervision. The zero-shot mode achieves accuracy comparable to a pre-trained model. The architecture generalises to any PDE admitting a convolutional finite-difference discretisation. Because latent dynamics are governed by a known PDE, SAFE-PIT-CM is inherently explainable: every prediction is traceable to a physical transport coefficient and step-by-step PDE propagation.

</details>


### [108] [ICaRus: Identical Cache Reuse for Efficient Multi Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13281)
*Sunghyeon Woo,Jaeeun Kil,Hoseung Kim,Minsub Kim,Joonghoon Kim,Ahreum Seo,Sungjae Lee,Minjung Jo,Jiwon Ryu,Baeseong Park,Se Jung Kwon,Dongsoo Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ICaRus提出一种多模型共享相同KV缓存的架构，通过将Transformer分解为逻辑编码器和解码器，冻结编码器并微调解码器，实现跨模型缓存复用，解决多模型推理中的内存爆炸和重复计算问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模型推理场景中，每个模型需要为相同提示维护独立的KV缓存，导致内存消耗剧增。缓存爆炸迫使系统驱逐缓存，重新计算时产生显著开销。此外，不同模型间无法共享前缀缓存，导致相同提示的重复计算。

Method: 将解码器Transformer概念分解为生成KV缓存的逻辑编码器和基于缓存预测输出令牌的逻辑解码器。冻结逻辑编码器，仅微调逻辑解码器，使多个模型共享相同的KV缓存。结合LoRA等轻量适配器，在解码时并行化KV缓存生成和下一令牌预测。

Result: ICaRus在多样化任务上达到与任务特定微调模型相当的准确率，同时允许多个专用模型完全共享KV缓存。在包含8个不同模型的多智能体工作流中，相比传统多模型系统，P95延迟降低11.1倍，吞吐量提高3.8倍。

Conclusion: ICaRus通过跨模型共享KV缓存，有效解决了多模型推理中的内存爆炸和重复计算问题，实现了高效且可扩展的多模型推理系统，显著提升了延迟和吞吐性能。

Abstract: Multi model inference has recently emerged as a prominent paradigm, particularly in the development of agentic AI systems. However, in such scenarios, each model must maintain its own Key-Value (KV) cache for the identical prompt, leading to substantial memory consumption. This explosive growth of KV caches forces LLM serving systems to evict previously stored caches, which in turn introduces significant recomputation overhead whenever the evicted caches are required again. Moreover, prefix caching is inherently infeasible across different models, forcing each model to recompute KV cache for the identical prompt, which leads to significant overhead. To alleviate these issues, we propose Identical Cache Reuse (ICaRus), a novel architecture that allows multiple models to share identical KV caches across all layers. ICaRus is based on the key observation that a decoder-only Transformer can be conceptually decomposed into a logical encoder, which generates KV caches, and a logical decoder, which predicts output tokens from the KV caches. ICaRus fine-tunes only the logical decoder while freezing the logical encoder, enabling multiple models to share an identical KV cache. This eliminates cache memory explosion and unexpected evictions while also allowing cross-model reuse of KV caches for new input tokens, thereby removing redundant recomputation in multi model inference achieving both efficiency and scalability. Moreover, by incorporating lightweight adapters such as LoRA, ICaRus parallelizes KV cache generation and next-token prediction during decoding. ICaRus achieves comparable accuracy to task-specific fine-tuned model across a diverse set of tasks, while allowing multiple specialized models to fully share KV caches. ICaRus achieves up to 11.1x lower P95 latency and 3.8x higher throughput in multi agent workflow with 8 different models, compared to conventional multi model system.

</details>


### [109] [FedTreeLoRA: Reconciling Statistical and Functional Heterogeneity in Federated LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13282)
*Jieming Bian,Lei Wang,Letian Zhang,Jie Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedTreeLoRA：一种用于联邦学习的树状聚合框架，通过分层参数共享解决LLM微调中的统计异质性和功能异质性耦合问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化联邦学习方法主要处理客户端统计异质性，但忽略了LLM层间的功能异质性。统计（水平）和功能（垂直）维度在来源上是正交的，但在交互中耦合，这意味着最优参数共享深度与客户端相似度功能相关。

Method: 提出FedTreeLoRA框架，采用树状结构聚合实现细粒度、分层对齐。通过动态构建聚合层次结构，使客户端能够在浅层"主干"上共享广泛共识，同时在深层"分支"上逐步专业化。

Result: 在NLU和NLG基准测试中，FedTreeLoRA显著优于最先进方法，有效平衡了泛化性和个性化需求。

Conclusion: FedTreeLoRA通过树状聚合框架解决了联邦学习中统计异质性和功能异质性的耦合问题，实现了更优的泛化-个性化权衡，为隐私保护的LLM微调提供了新思路。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) with Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has become a standard for privacy-preserving LLM fine-tuning. However, existing personalized methods predominantly operated under a restrictive Flat-Model Assumption: they addressed client-side \textit{statistical heterogeneity} but treated the model as a monolithic block, ignoring the \textit{functional heterogeneity} across LLM layers. We argue that these two statistical (horizontal) and functional (vertical) dimensions, are \textit{orthogonal in source yet coupled in interaction}, implying that the optimal depth of parameter sharing is functionally dependent on client similarity. To address this, we propose \textbf{FedTreeLoRA}, a framework employing tree-structured aggregation for fine-grained, layer-wise alignment. By dynamically constructing an aggregation hierarchy, FedTreeLoRA allows clients to share broad consensus on shallow `trunks' while progressively specializing on deep `branches'. Experiments on NLU and NLG benchmarks demonstrate that FedTreeLoRA significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods by effectively reconciling generalization and personalization.

</details>


### [110] [Do Diffusion Models Dream of Electric Planes? Discrete and Continuous Simulation-Based Inference for Aircraft Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13284)
*Aurelien Ghiglino,Daniel Elenius,Anirban Roy,Ramneet Kaur,Manoj Acharya,Colin Samplawski,Brian Matejek,Susmit Jha,Juan Alonso,Adam Cobb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于仿真推理的分层扩散模型方法，用于生成电动垂直起降飞行器的概念工程设计


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要高效生成eVTOL飞机的概念工程设计，传统方法耗时且难以探索完整设计空间

Method: 采用分层概率模型：第一个模型基于黎曼扩散语言建模和统一世界模型采样离散拓扑结构；第二个模型使用掩码扩散方法在给定拓扑下采样连续参数

Result: 方法能够重新发现飞机设计中已知的趋势和物理定律，同时显著加速设计生成过程

Conclusion: 提出的分层扩散模型框架有效支持eVTOL概念工程设计，实现了设计空间的快速探索和物理规律的自动发现

Abstract: In this paper, we generate conceptual engineering designs of electric vertical take-off and landing (eVTOL) aircraft. We follow the paradigm of simulation-based inference (SBI), whereby we look to learn a posterior distribution over the full eVTOL design space. To learn this distribution, we sample over discrete aircraft configurations (topologies) and their corresponding set of continuous parameters. Therefore, we introduce a hierarchical probabilistic model consisting of two diffusion models. The first model leverages recent work on Riemannian Diffusion Language Modeling (RDLM) and Unified World Models (UWMs) to enable us to sample topologies from a discrete and continuous space. For the second model we introduce a masked diffusion approach to sample the corresponding parameters conditioned on the topology. Our approach rediscovers known trends and governing physical laws in aircraft design, while significantly accelerating design generation.

</details>


### [111] [Brittlebench: Quantifying LLM robustness via prompt sensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13285)
*Angelika Romanou,Mark Ibrahim,Candace Ross,Chantal Shaib,Kerem Okta,Sam Bell,Elia Ovalle,Jesse Dodge,Antoine Bosselut,Koustuv Sinha,Adina Williams*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Brittlebench评估框架，通过语义保持的提示扰动量化语言模型对输入变异的敏感性（脆弱性），揭示传统基准测试高估模型真实性能的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法依赖干净、静态的基准测试，无法捕捉真实世界用户输入中的噪声和变异性（如拼写错误、不同表达方式），导致高估模型性能。需要量化模型对提示变异的敏感性，区分数据难度和提示相关变异性。

Method: 提出理论框架量化模型对提示变异的敏感性（脆弱性），设计Brittlebench评估流水线：对流行基准测试应用语义保持的扰动，系统评估前沿模型对输入变异的敏感性。

Result: 语义保持扰动导致模型性能下降高达12%；单个扰动在63%情况下改变模型相对排名；输入扰动可解释高达50%的模型性能方差；开源和商业模型均受影响。

Conclusion: Brittlebench揭示了模型对输入变异的显著脆弱性，强调需要更稳健的评估方法和模型设计，为系统理解模型脆弱性提供工具。

Abstract: Existing evaluation methods largely rely on clean, static benchmarks, which can overestimate true model performance by failing to capture the noise and variability inherent in real-world user inputs. This is especially true for language models, which can face human-generated text queries containing mistakes, typos, or alternative ways of phrasing the same question. In this work, we introduce a theoretical framework for quantifying model sensitivity to prompt variants, or brittleness, that can enable us to disentangle data-induced difficulty from prompt-related variability. Using this framework, we design a novel evaluation pipeline, Brittlebench, to holistically evaluate the sensitivity of frontier models. We apply semantics-preserving perturbations to a suite of popular benchmarks, and observe model performance to degrade as much as 12%. However, these perturbations do not affect all models equally: even a single perturbation alters the relative ranking of models in 63% of cases, impacting conclusions about comparative model performance. Decomposing the total variance of both state-of-the-art open-weight and commercial models, we find that semantics-preserving input perturbations can account for up to half of the performance variance for a given model. Brittlebench highlights the need for more robust evaluations and models, and allows us to systematically understand model brittleness.

</details>


### [112] [From Stochastic Answers to Verifiable Reasoning: Interpretable Decision-Making with LLM-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13287)
*Anirudh Jaidev Mahesh,Ben Griffin,Fuat Alican,Joseph Ternasky,Zakari Salifu,Kelvin Amoaba,Yagiz Ihlamur,Aaron Ontoyin Yin,Aikins Laryea,Afriyie Samuel,Yigit Ihlamur*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLM作为代码生成器而非逐样本评估器，生成可执行决策逻辑，实现可扩展、可解释、可复现的高风险决策


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM决策方法难以兼顾可扩展性、可解释性和可复现性：黑盒模型缺乏透明度，基于规则的LLM系统需要逐样本评估导致成本随数据集规模增长，且输出具有随机性和幻觉风险

Method: 将LLM重构为代码生成器而非逐实例评估器，单次LLM调用生成可执行、人类可读的决策逻辑；结合自动统计验证（精度提升、二项显著性检验、覆盖过滤）和基于聚类的差距分析迭代优化决策逻辑，无需人工标注

Result: 在VCBench基准测试（4,500名创始人，9%基础成功率）上，该方法达到37.5%精度和25.0% F0.5分数，优于GPT-4o（30.0%精度，25.7% F0.5分数），同时保持完全可解释性

Conclusion: 通过将LLM作为代码生成器生成可执行决策规则，实现了可验证、可解释的LLM决策系统，解决了高风险决策中可扩展性、可解释性和可复现性的平衡问题

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for high-stakes decision-making, yet existing approaches struggle to reconcile scalability, interpretability, and reproducibility. Black-box models obscure their reasoning, while recent LLM-based rule systems rely on per-sample evaluation, causing costs to scale with dataset size and introducing stochastic, hallucination-prone outputs. We propose reframing LLMs as code generators rather than per-instance evaluators. A single LLM call generates executable, human-readable decision logic that runs deterministically over structured data, eliminating per-sample LLM queries while enabling reproducible and auditable predictions. We combine code generation with automated statistical validation using precision lift, binomial significance testing, and coverage filtering, and apply cluster-based gap analysis to iteratively refine decision logic without human annotation. We instantiate this framework in venture capital founder screening, a rare-event prediction task with strong interpretability requirements. On VCBench, a benchmark of 4,500 founders with a 9% base success rate, our approach achieves 37.5% precision and an F0.5 score of 25.0%, outperforming GPT-4o (at 30.0% precision and an F0.5 score of 25.7%) while maintaining full interpretability. Each prediction traces to executable rules over human-readable attributes, demonstrating verifiable and interpretable LLM-based decision-making in practice.

</details>


### [113] [Learning Enhanced Structural Representations with Block-Based Uncertainties for Ocean Floor Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.14372)
*Jose Marie Antonio Minoza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于空间块和VQ-VAE的不确定性感知机制，用于高分辨率海底地形重建，通过块状保形预测捕捉局部复杂性，提供空间自适应置信估计


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前全球海底地形数据集分辨率不足，无法满足精确数值模拟需求；现有深度学习方法在保持物理结构一致性和量化不确定性方面存在挑战

Method: 基于块状保形预测的不确定性感知机制，结合VQ-VAE架构，利用离散潜在表示保持地形特征，通过空间块设计自适应捕捉局部海底复杂性

Result: 在多个海区实验中，相比传统技术，在重建质量和不确定性估计可靠性方面均有显著提升，在特征明确区域提供较小不确定性宽度，在复杂海底结构区域提供适当较大的边界

Conclusion: 该框架通过保持结构完整性并提供空间自适应不确定性估计，提高了海底地形重建的可靠性，为更稳健的气候建模和海岸灾害评估开辟了新途径

Abstract: Accurate ocean modeling and coastal hazard prediction depend on high-resolution bathymetric data; yet, current worldwide datasets are too coarse for exact numerical simulations. While recent deep learning advances have improved earth observation data resolution, existing methods struggle with the unique challenges of producing detailed ocean floor maps, especially in maintaining physical structure consistency and quantifying uncertainties. This work presents a novel uncertainty-aware mechanism using spatial blocks to efficiently capture local bathymetric complexity based on block-based conformal prediction. Using the Vector Quantized Variational Autoencoder (VQ-VAE) architecture, the integration of this uncertainty quantification framework yields spatially adaptive confidence estimates while preserving topographical features via discrete latent representations. With smaller uncertainty widths in well-characterized areas and appropriately larger bounds in areas of complex seafloor structures, the block-based design adapts uncertainty estimates to local bathymetric complexity. Compared to conventional techniques, experimental results over several ocean regions show notable increases in both reconstruction quality and uncertainty estimation reliability. This framework increases the reliability of bathymetric reconstructions by preserving structural integrity while offering spatially adaptive uncertainty estimates, so opening the path for more solid climate modeling and coastal hazard assessment.

</details>
